# Linux fontconfig golden (FontConfigManager / fontpath_linux.c)
# Captured from the JNI build of commit 7b43255b30 (libjavafx_font.so,
# libjavafx_font_freetype.so, libjavafx_font_pango.so) before any of it was rewritten.
# Machine-specific: the values depend on the installed fonts, FreeType, fontconfig, Pango and
# HarfBuzz. The comparison runs only where every machine.* key matches; elsewhere it is skipped,
# or failed under -Djfx.parity.require=true.
#
# Capture, in a fresh clone of 7b43255b30 whose JNI libraries are built from source:
#   mvn -B -ntp -pl modules/javafx.graphics -am test -Dtest='Linux*GoldenTest' -Dsurefire.failIfNoSpecifiedTests=false -Djfx.font.golden.capture=true
# Regenerate: add -Djfx.font.golden.regenerate=true (reviewed as a behaviour change)
#
# Keys:
#   machine.*    gate: os, library and font sha256, fontconfig inventory and configuration,
#                environment, locale, properties
#   info.*       recorded, never compared (library versions, package versions, corpus text)
#   capture.*    recorded, never compared (JVM, commit, JNI libraries)
#   #null        a null value; u:XXXX.XXXX is text as UTF-16 code units
#   fc.raw.gfc.<locale>.<fb|nofb>  getFontConfig(locale, 12 logical names, includeFallbacks):
#                          .return; .eNN.name/.first (index of firstFont in allFonts by identity)/
#                          .firstFont/.count; .eNN.fMMM = family;style;fullName;file
#   fc.raw.gfc.partial.*   the first candidate FcNameParse rejects, between two good names
#   fc.raw.pm.<locale>.*   populateMapsNative into fresh maps: .return; .counts (file;family;list);
#                          .file.<key>, .family.<key>, .list.<key> (list in insertion order)
#   fc.raw.pm.seeded.*     populateMapsNative into pre-seeded maps
#   fc.view.gfcf.<family>.<style>  getFontConfigFont: fcName|fcFamily|style|firstFont;
#                          .files/.names = getFileNames/getFontNames
#   fc.api.*               PrismFontFactory family and full name lists; getFallbacks names|files
#   fc.logical.<family>.<style>  uncached LogicalFont: slot 0 full name|file|number of slots
